## Claims and anchors

- C67 added: The order of experience matters because it changes which defects become visible and which refinements become available before Bayesian judgment resumes.
- C68 added: The question-history channel of strategic heterogeneity does not require heterogeneity to begin with different priors; the model also permits cognitive-style differences in detection capacity and switching hurdles, but question-dependent representation change is the distinctive source.
- C69 added: Two firms facing the same opportunity may not disagree over common theories at all: one may possess an actionable distinction the other has never formulated, so comparing priors starts too late.
- C70 added: The same logic integrates anomaly tolerance and cessation: a firm may rationally leave a question unasked when leverage is zero, stop after its achievability weight decays, or abandon a record no admissible refinement can repair.
- C71 added: Success can remove action-separating leverage, repeated production failure can reduce the assessed chance of resolution, and a genuine anomaly can remain economically dormant when no feasible refinement changes robust action. Questions can therefore die through success, discouragement, or irreparability even though the underlying causal structure remains unresolved.
- C72 added: The relevant contribution of an outside warrant assessor is diagnostic variation separating worlds the focal agent cannot jointly represent, so governance is epistemically useful only when the outsider's broader language and evidence support that discrimination.
- C73 added: A strategically deviant actor functions as epistemic infrastructure only when its variation exposes the coarse language; strong customer outside options can instead discipline a consensus, while forgiving demand can protect it.
- C74 added: A false theory may predict well along the path it induces because its policy suppresses the contrast that would refute it, yet the same theory can fail after a rival differentiates or customers visibly punish misservice. Concealment is only protection: changes in play or subsequent inquiry can still unsettle the represented class.
- C75 added: Once refinement and formulation create a new represented class, conventional interventions and Bayesian discrimination can again operate among its members.
- C76 added: In the deterministic case judged updating coincides with ordinary Bayesian conditioning while the Bayesian normalizer is positive; a zero-likelihood contradiction can instead make ordinary conditioning itself undefined.
- C77 added: In the stochastic model, false rejection and irreparable finite records mean that dissonance is an operational diagnosis, not by itself proof of objective representational insufficiency: causal dissonance is the model's operational signal that no admitted theory survives the current evidence, and in the stochastic setting it is not by itself proof that the objective truth is inexpressible.
- C78 added: Because the repair test applies to every nonempty recoded cell-profile pair, refinement can reconcile observations but cannot delete their constraints.
- C79 added: When the record is irreparable, neither response (electing a doomed inquiry while the deliberative record admits a positive-leverage coherent grouping, or declining immediately when no coherent grouping prices the question) requires the agent to recognize analyst-level irreparability.
- C80 added: The family $\mathcal G_i(r_i)$ of coherent groupings may be empty under stochastic sampling: full support guarantees positive likelihood, not finite-sample passage of the consistency test.
- C81 added: The participation rule is a coherent non-Bayesian criterion operating precisely where the acting belief is undefined, and it respects every discipline of section 4.6: $\widehat B_i$ quantifies over the agent's own remembered tokens and the groupings it can articulate, never over named future representations.
- C82 added: Enlightened inertia remains a second source of persistence, distinct from an unpriced question, whether or not mechanisms are deterministic, and it feeds the performance differences of section 10.
- C83 added: In the running example, defeat concentrates the evidence, dissonance, and incentive in the losing firm: competitive defeat concentrates diagnostic evidence and produces a winner/loser inquiry asymmetry, which the example exhibits without implying that losers generally have greater inquiry incentives.
- C84 added: Part (d) of Theorem 10.4 and the rest-point characterization are conditional on play stabilizing, which in every configuration featured in this paper is immediate: their beliefs make one action per cell strictly dominant.
- C85 added: The question-history source of strategic heterogeneity complements rather than replaces familiar explanations based on heterogeneous resources, incentives, beliefs, or capabilities.
- C86 added: A broader-awareness board, investor, advisor, or other outsider can serve as a warrant assessor if it represents the alternatives and has an appropriate assessment model; outside challenge can therefore play a warrant role under those conditions.
- C1 text: "Existing models show how theories guide learning, how Bayesian agents discriminate among represented causal structures, and how play can reveal previously unavailable actions. This paper models a different transition: an agent's causal language pools objectively different situations; strategic experience endogenously reveals an action-relevant failure of that pooling and thereby creates a localized open question; costly inquiry activates a fallible technology whose output is not measurable in the agent's prior awareness; only after that output is formulated does the agent construct a prior and undertake Bayesian judgment, under an explicit account of how much confidence the generating experience licenses. Because actions depend on theories, play can either create the contrasts that generate questions or suppress them, so the model also delivers the population-level answer: when theory heterogeneity and performance differences persist, and when the market erases them." → 'Existing work explains learning and action once theories, vocabularies, or awareness advantages exist; this paper supplies the pre-Bayesian passage from represented-class failure to a question, a fallible new representation, formulation, and renewed Bayesian judgment. Its distinctive economic and epistemic results are that an agent can value a finer actionable distinction before representing its answers, and that evidence generating a truth-blind representation cannot be counted again as independent validation. Embedding that passage in strategic interaction provides a mechanism by which play exposes or conceals coarse languages and establishes the existence of absorbing configurations with heterogeneous terminal languages and payoffs. The contribution is therefore upstream: question histories and rational stopping points can generate strategic heterogeneity before priors over the resulting theories are available. The paper endogenizes the strategically consequential transition into a new hypothesis space while leaving the semantic act that produces it primitive.'
- C1 evidence pointer: 'paper/paper.md:L41-L160' → 'paper/paper.md:L1171-L1200'
- C10 text: "Proposition 6.6 (Robust sufficiency). A partition sufficient for every assignment in a candidate set T must refine the join over tau' in T of P^tau', the join of the candidates' causal-equivalence partitions. A representation guaranteed to accommodate all observationally equivalent worlds is accordingly finer than any single world requires; a technology that economizes on distinctions must accept fallibility." → "Example 1 (continued, Section 7): After discovery the record labels architecture 0 as preferred at s_00 and architecture 1 at s_11. Of the 15 partitions of the four states, exactly 4 are minimally restorative, each separating s_00 from s_11: the row partition {{s_00,s_01},{s_10,s_11}}, the column partition {{s_00,s_10},{s_01,s_11}}, and the two partitions isolating one labeled state. The record directs inquiry (11 partitions are excluded) without determining it (4 remain). A factor-seeking technology produces the row or the column refinement. The column world tau'(s_rc) = theta^c agrees with the row world on the full joint mechanism at both visited states, so no truth-blind rule can formulate correctly in both: a randomized rule succeeds in the worst case with probability at most 1/2. By Proposition 7.5 a partition sufficient for both worlds must be the full partition into singletons. Whichever factor refinement arrives, its unique consistent theory extrapolates the wrong prediction to the two unlabeled states in one of the two worlds."

- C11 text: "Example 1 (continued, section 6.3). After the dissonant generation the record contains two labeled episodes: architecture 0 objectively preferred at s00, architecture 1 at s11. Of the fifteen partitions of four states, exactly four are minimally restorative, each separating s00 from s11: the row partition {{s00, s01}, {s10, s11}}, the column partition {{s00, s10}, {s01, s11}}, and the two partitions isolating one labeled state; the record directs inquiry (eleven partitions are excluded) without determining it (four remain). Under a complexity ordering counting the coordinates needed to describe a refinement, a factor-seeking technology produces the row or the column refinement. The column world tau'(s_rc) = theta^c agrees with the row world at both labeled states, so the two worlds generate identical records; no truth-blind rule can formulate correctly in both, a randomized rule succeeds in the worst case with probability at most one-half, and a partition sufficient for both worlds must be the full partition into singletons. Whichever factor refinement arrives, its unique consistent theory extrapolates the wrong prediction to the two unlabeled states in one of the two worlds." → 'Proposition 8.4 (Participation): B_hat_i(r_i) is well defined, nonnegative, bounded by T*(max pi_i - min pi_i), and computable from the deliberative record, the standing policy, and the agent-known model primitives and test calibration. At a dissonant record, electing inquiry has represented value lambda_i^(k_i)*B_hat_i(r_i) - gamma_i, so agent i elects if and only if lambda_i^(k_i)*B_hat_i(r_i) >= gamma_i. Moreover, at a dissonant record, B_hat_i(r_i) > 0 if and only if J_i(G; r_i) > 0 for some G in G_i(r_i). Thus a question is priced positively only when some coherent reading of the record supports action variation within at least one current awareness cell that robustly outperforms the best action kept uniform on that cell under the same coherent reading.'

- C12 text: 'Proposition 7.4 (Answer invariance and the participation rule). B_i(r_i) is well defined, computable from the deliberative record together with the known structure (Theta, u_i), and invariant across the elements of R(P_i, r_i): every Lambda_i-measurable rule is implementable after any successful production, and the maximand of equation (6) makes no reference to which repair arrives. Moreover B_i(r_i) >= 0. Electing inquiry has represented value lambda_i B_i(r_i) - gamma_i, so agent i elects if and only if lambda_i B_i(r_i) >= gamma_i; in particular, elections occur only at dissonant records that admit some repair. Finally, B_i(r_i) > 0 if and only if some pinned rule strictly and robustly improves on the carried-forward policy: questions whose every admissible answer supports current behavior are worthless at any positive cost, however much model multiplicity remains.' → 'Example 1 (continued, Section 8): At the discovery record (T = 2, diagonal states served), deterministic conflicting payoffs certify the two tokens as distinct states, and the best coherent grouping splits them. The fine optimal gain is 0 on the winning token and 1/2 on the losing token, so L_i^fine = 1/2, L_i^cur = 0, J_i = 1/2 and B_hat_i = (2/2)*(1/2) = 1/2 for both firms. Each elects iff lambda_i^(0)*B_hat_i >= gamma_i, i.e. lambda_i^(0)/2 >= gamma_i. After the row refinement, the post-refinement horizon gain is exactly 1/2, so the firm re-differentiates when kappa_i < 1/2 and holds its old policy (enlightened inertia) when kappa_i >= 1/2. In a later stretch serving the two off-diagonal states, firm 1 holds the row refinement and firm 2 the column refinement. Then L_1^fine = L_1^cur = J_1 = B_hat_1 = 0 (the firm holding the truth never inquires again), while L_2^fine = 1, L_2^cur = 0, J_2 = 1 and B_hat_2 = (2/4)*1 = 1/2. The winner/loser asymmetry B_hat_1 = 0 < B_hat_2 is the durable point.'

- C13 text: "Example 1 (continued, section 7). At the dissonant record both firms' committed constant policies disagree with exactly one revealed label (firm 1 lost at s11, firm 2 at s00). Every restorative refinement separates the two labeled tokens, and implementing a token's revealed label at its recurrence gains exactly one-half against every rival action: if the rival plays the winning architecture the switch converts a differentiated loss (0) into a pooled half; if the rival switches, it converts a pooled half into a differentiated win (1). Hence B_i = 1/2 for both firms, and each elects inquiry if and only if lambda_i/2 >= gamma_i; the valuation uses no conjecture about the rival and no reference to which of the four admissible refinements might arrive, Theta_i(t) is a singleton, and the bound is exact. After a validation generation in which firm 1 has acquired the row refinement (certain of the true row assignment) and firm 2 the column refinement (certain of the false column assignment), their policies differ at the two off-diagonal states, firm 1 wins both, and the labels revealed there falsify firm 2's assignment: B_1 = 0, so the firm holding the truth never inquires again and its awareness remains permanently incomplete; B_2 = 1, so firm 2 elects whenever lambda_2 >= gamma_2 and, upon success, is driven stepwise to complete awareness." → "Theorem 9.2 (No additional evidence beyond the generating record): Conditional on the analyst record r_bar_i and the pre-production inquiry history h_i, a record-measurable, truth-blind production, formulation, and bridge-prior-selection rule makes the complete output O_i = (P_i', m', mu_i^+) conditionally independent of the candidate objective assignment. Consequently, under the reflective joint law, for K_i(.|r_bar_i, h_i)-almost every realized output o and every tau' in calT, nu(tau' | r_bar_i, h_i, O_i = o) = nu(tau' | r_bar_i, h_i), and therefore W(o) = nu(A_o | r_bar_i, h_i). Passing the repair test adds no information beyond the record. If candidates assign equal likelihood to (r_bar_i, h_i), their posterior odds remain their reflective prior odds."

- C14 text: "Theorem 8.2 (The insight is not evidence). If the technology is truth-blind, conditioning on the production event adds nothing to the record: W equals the ordinary reflective posterior of m''s correctness given the record alone. Post-restoration fit is guaranteed by the restoration property, so passing the test carries no information beyond the record either. Among candidates in T that induce the same record distribution, prior odds are preserved exactly." → "Corollary 9.3 (Self-audit requires sufficiency for the candidates): The assessment (calT, nu) is expressible by agent i only if its current post-refinement partition P_i' is sufficient for every tau' in calT, hence only if P_i' refines the join of Proposition 7.5. This is necessary but not sufficient for complete computation of warrant. An agent holding a minimal restoration therefore generally cannot formulate the cross-representation comparison required to determine the sign or magnitude of its signed warrant gap; a broader-awareness assessor may be able to."

- C15 text: "Corollary 8.3 (Self-audit requires sufficiency for the candidates). The assessment (T, nu) is expressible by agent i only if P_i is sufficient for every tau' in T, hence only if P_i refines the join of Proposition 6.6. An agent holding a minimal restoration therefore generally cannot formulate the comparison that measures its own warrant gap: the overconfidence is invisible from within the representation that produces it. Short of that refinement, warrant must be supplied externally, by fresh evidence whose likelihoods differ across candidates, by an assessor with broader awareness, or by a future self." → "Example 1 (continued, Section 9): If firm i's technology produces the column refinement, the recoded record pins the unique consistent theory (architecture 0 on the first column, 1 on the second), and representation-relative coherence assigns it probability 1. With calT = {row world, column world} and a symmetric prior, the two worlds assign the realized diagonal-only record equal probability and the technology is truth-blind. So by Theorem 9.2, W(O_i) = 1/2 and the signed warrant gap is 1 - 1/2 = +1/2. The same gap applies to a firm that acquires the row refinement (coherence 1, warrant 1/2): confidence exceeds warrant even when the formulated theory is objectively correct. By Corollary 9.3 neither firm can conduct this assessment itself. One fresh differentiated observation at an off-diagonal state resolves the row/column identification problem completely; at beta = 1, any number of pooled observations remains nondiagnostic."

- C16 text: "Example 1 (continued, section 8). Suppose firm i's technology produces the column refinement. The recoded record pins the unique consistent theory (architecture 0 on the first column, 1 on the second) and representation-relative coherence assigns it probability one. In the reflective assessment with T = {row world, column world} and a symmetric prior, the two worlds generate identical records and the technology is truth-blind, so the reflective posterior equals the prior: W = 1/2, and the warrant gap is exactly one-half. The same gap afflicts the firm that acquires the row refinement: its coherence in the true assignment is also probability one and its warrant is also one-half. Neither firm can conduct this assessment itself, since expressing both candidate worlds requires the full partition into singletons. One fresh differentiated observation at an off-diagonal state closes the gap completely (the two worlds predict opposite labels there), while pooled observations, in any number, close nothing." → "Theorem 10.4 (Epistemic absorption): Under Assumption 10.3, almost surely: (a) at most n*(|S| - 1) refinements occur, and every agent's awareness partition is eventually constant; (b) only finitely many inquiry elections occur, because each agent stops electing once its achievability weight has decayed enough that lambda_i^(k)*T*(max pi_i - min pi_i) < gamma_i, which happens after finitely many consecutive failures because B_hat_i is bounded and gamma_i > 0; (c) there is an almost surely finite period after which no agent's language changes and no agent pays an inquiry cost, and every agent's epistemic life thereafter is judged-Bayesian updating within a fixed language. Moreover, on the event that the standing profile is eventually constant: (d) every agent's active set is eventually constant; every retained theory is eta-adequate at every recurrent pair under terminal play; every theory eta-inadequate at a recurrent pair is eventually absent; and each agent is eventually either never dissonant or in dissonant stasis."

- C17 text: "Theorem 9.4 (Epistemic absorption). Under Assumption 9.3, almost surely: (a) at most n(|S| - 1) refinements occur, and every agent's awareness partition is eventually constant; (b) only finitely many inquiry elections occur; and (c) there is an almost surely finite generation after which no agent's language changes and no agent pays an inquiry cost: every agent's epistemic life thereafter is judged-Bayesian updating within a fixed language, punctuated at most by dissonance episodes that fail the participation threshold. Moreover, on the event that the committed profile is eventually constant, (d) every agent's active set is eventually constant, every theory it contains is eta-adequate under the terminal play at every recurrent observation pair, and each agent is eventually either never dissonant (it lives in a Bayesian world) or in dissonant stasis: dissonance recurs while lambda_i B_i < gamma_i, in particular whenever B_i = 0, as at irreparable records (R(P_i, r_i) = emptyset)." → 'Proposition D.5 (Rest points and persistence), as stated in the paper: converging non-stasis configurations limit to a partial SCUE. A SCUE configuration with strict optimality margins and zero leverage persists, exactly with deterministic mechanisms and with probability at least 1 - sum_i exp(-b_i) in the stochastic case, so capacity governs the robustness of a rest point.'

- C18 text: "Proposition 9.5 (Rest points and persistence). (i) If awareness partitions, committed policies, conjectures, and acting beliefs are all eventually constant almost surely, then the limiting configuration, restricted to the agents not in dissonant stasis, is a self-confirming unawareness equilibrium. (ii) Conversely, consider a configuration whose epistemic states satisfy the SCUE conditions, whose play generates only records with B_i = 0, and in which each agent's committed action at each cell is strictly optimal under every conjecture and every theory in its active set. (a) With deterministic mechanisms, the configuration persists: dissonance, elections, and policy changes never occur, and the SCUE conditions continue to hold, with conjectures converging empirically to induced play. (b) With stochastic mechanisms, suppose in addition that every theory in every active set is eta'-adequate under the configuration's play for some eta' < eta, and that the strict optimality extends to every (eta + epsilon)-adequate theory for some epsilon in (0, 2(eta - eta')]. Then for every delta > 0 there is a count threshold N(delta) such that, if the configuration is reached with at least N(delta) observations at every recurrent pair, then with probability at least 1 - delta play never changes, dissonance and elections never occur, and the SCUE conditions hold in every subsequent generation, with conjectures converging empirically to induced play." → 'Proposition D.6 (On-path adequacy, and where error lives), as stated in the paper: in a SCUE, surviving error lives only in what play never exposes. Acting-belief mass on every eta-inadequate theory vanishes, and all retained falsity is confined to pooled mechanism assignments within tolerance at visited profiles and to counterfactual claims at unvisited profiles.'

- C19 text: 'Proposition 9.8 (On-path adequacy, and where error lives). In a SCUE, every theory in the support of every mu_i is eta-adequate at every visited pair. All surviving falsity is confined to what the configuration does not expose: mechanism assignments at states pooled with causally different states whose mixture imitates a single mechanism at the visited profiles, and counterfactual claims at unvisited profiles.' → 'Proposition D.7 (Capacity: detection speed and robustness), as stated in the paper: in stochastic environments capacity is a single cognitive-style dial. Expected detection time under an exposed profile is O(b_i/D_i) (and Omega(b_i)), while spurious dissonance at a non-exposed rest point has probability at most exp(-b_i). A larger b_i lowers false-alarm risk but requires more accumulated evidence to detect a broken language, and capacity plays no role in the exact deterministic case.'

There exist primitives and absorbing configurations in which agents hold different terminal partitions and theories, expected per-generation payoffs differ strictly and permanently, and no disadvantaged agent has a priced repair: for each such agent either B_i = 0 or lambda_i B_i < gamma_i. The proof is by the instances of Example 1.' → 'Proposition 10.5 (Persistent performance differences): There exist primitives and absorbing configurations in which agents hold different terminal partitions and theories, and expected per-period payoffs differ strictly and permanently, with no disadvantaged agent closing the gap. This is because, for each, the corrective process has died economically at one of its two stages: either its question is unpriced (dissonant stasis, lambda_i^(k_i)*B_hat_i(r_i) < gamma_i from some period on), or, having asked and refined, the policy change its new model recommends is priced below the switching hurdle (enlightened inertia, T-horizon gain <= kappa_i).'

- C22 text: 'Example 1 (continued, section 9, the trap and its knife edge). Set beta = 1 and suppose both firms acquired the column refinement after the discovery generation and became certain of the false column assignment. - C24 text: 'Bayesian learning operates only within the current language, and a misspecified posterior grows confident rather than alarmed, so detecting representational failure requires a distinct faculty: causal dissonance, the statistical rejection of every expressible theory. Bayesian updating cannot expand the language and cannot even detect that expansion is needed; detection is statistical dissonance, a faculty distinct from the posterior.' → 'Confidence can grow even as every expressible theory fails: updating does not sound an alarm when the space itself is broken, because posterior mass always sums to one, so a manager whose every expressible theory is false can grow steadily more confident in the least false among them.'

- C25 text: "When every represented theory is false, the posterior does not sound an alarm: by the logic of misspecified Bayesian learning (Berk 1966), it concentrates on the least wrong of the available theories, and the agent grows more confident, not less; within-language updating is self-sealing. In the stochastic setting (Remark 4.2), when every expressible theory is false because the agent's cell mixes mechanisms, a full-support posterior does not signal this: it concentrates on the expressible theories closest to the on-path conditionals in Kullback–Leibler divergence, and the agent becomes increasingly *confident* in a wrong theory. Posterior mass always sums to one; the inadequacy of the whole model class is not an event in the agent's algebra, so detecting that the language itself is broken requires a faculty outside the posterior—the consistency test." → 'Experience directs repair without determining it (without selecting its answer), so insight, economical representation change, is necessarily fallible: the requirement that a successful repair reconcile the retained record directs inquiry without determining its output.'

- C26 text: "Dissonance directs inquiry without determining its output. - C27 text: 'Questions die rationally: sustained success extinguishes them, and defeat concentrates both the evidence and the incentive to inquire in the losing firm. - C28 text: 'The return to Bayes is disciplined by a warrant account: because any successful repair fits the experience that generated it by construction, retrospective fit carries no information, and—when inquiry is truth-blind—neither does the arrival of the insight itself. - C29 text: "Because theories cause actions and actions generate the evidence, almost surely awareness change and inquiry die out, leaving every agent permanently inhabiting a Bayesian world—models fit, questions are dead, inquiry is priced below cost. The configurations in which the process rests, self-confirming unawareness equilibria, can preserve false theories, heterogeneous awareness (heterogeneous causal languages), and permanent performance differences, and they do so precisely when neither of the two corrective forces—strategic variety inside an agent's categories, or an environment harsh enough to punish misservice observably—is present: heterogeneous causal languages, and the performance differences they support, persist exactly when these strategic and environmental conditions fail to erase them." → 'Inquiry is an economic process, and it can die in every way an economic process dies: the question is answered, it is priced below its cost, it is abandoned after repeated failure, or it was never answerable.'

- C3 text: "Example 1 (continued, section 4). Under the coarse partition P_i^0 = {S}, firm i's expressible theories are the constant assignments m^0, m^1, m^n. - C30 text: 'Strategic variety is traced to its source: agents with identical preferences, identical data-processing (Bayesian) competence, and identical objective opportunities, facing the same objective system, end up with different causal languages, different theories, and different payoffs because their experiences posed different questions, their technologies produced different repairs, and their subsequent positions extinguished further inquiry at different points. - C43 text: 'Remark 6.3 (Irreparable records). Because $\\mathcal R$ is computable from the record, the agent recognizes irreparability; the technology fails surely; and the participation rule assigns the question no value, so no inquiry cost is wasted on it. - C54 text: 'Strategic variety has a pre-probabilistic source: agents with identical preferences, identical Bayesian competence, and identical objective opportunities diverge because their experiences posed different questions, their technologies produced different repairs, and their positions extinguished inquiry at different points. Heterogeneous prior understanding generates heterogeneous questions; questions generate heterogeneous languages; languages generate heterogeneous theories, plans, and payoffs. This sequence begins before probability does, and it is path-dependent in the strong sense: the order of experiences shapes the terminal language, and agents can converge to the same behavior while retaining permanently different representations.' → 'Switching costs temper the cycling of standing policies within a Bayesian world: the inaction band absorbs belief fluctuations too small to be worth acting on, so only swings whose horizon-$T$ gain exceeds $\\kappa_i$ move play, a stabilizing force, though not one that rules out large-amplitude cycles.'

- C55 text: "The value of experimentation is not information: a question is worth asking when some admissible answer would change action, not when its resolution would be most informative. High-entropy uncertainty with no action leverage is rationally ignored, and narrow contrasts that straddle an action threshold are rationally pursued. This explains rational cessation—why successful firms stop asking—and the loser's advantage in inquiry: competitive defeat concentrates the evidence, the dissonance, and the incentive in the defeated firm." → 'Abandoned questions are a substantive absorbing outcome, not an artifact: inquiry ceases because it is answered, because it is unpriced, or because it is given up.'

- C56 text: "Early confidence outruns evidence even when correct. A founder's theory that beautifully organizes the experiences that generated it has, by Theorem 8.2, earned nothing by that organization: the fit was guaranteed by construction. Validation requires evidence whose likelihood differs across the candidate worlds, and, by Corollary 8.3, the founder typically cannot even express the comparison, which is a formal argument for outside challenge (boards, investors, advisors with different awareness) as a warrant technology rather than a monitoring technology." → "Were $\\lambda_i^{(k)}$ held fixed instead of decaying, an agent could elect a doomed inquiry forever, paying $\\gamma_i$ each period for a repair that never arrives; the decay rules this out and is the model's account of when a rational inquirer stops."
- C56 evidence pointer: 'paper/paper.md:L1038-L1116' → 'paper/paper.md#rem-scope'
- C57 text: "Markets discipline theories through two channels: payoff-relevant collective error requires both concealment by play and forgiveness by demand. Industries where customers absorb misservice—captive buyers, no substitutes, third parties bearing the cost—can sustain shared false theories indefinitely; industries with strong outside options discipline theories even under consensus. Where demand is forgiving, the only corrective is strategic variety in beliefs, which gives belief heterogeneity a social value distinct from its private value: the entrant with a deviant theory is the industry's epistemic infrastructure." → "Zero-leverage dissonant stasis is a recognizable condition: the agent registers that no admitted theory survives its test, yet its deliberative valuation finds no coherent grouping with positive representational increment, so repairing the account is worth nothing to it; anomaly tolerance is rational precisely when the agent's coherent groupings carry no gross representational leverage."
- C57 evidence pointer: 'paper/paper.md:L1306-L1410' → 'paper/paper.md#prop-participation'
- C58 text: 'Anomaly tolerance is an equilibrium phenomenon: firms that know their causal account is broken and rationally decline to fix it are not failing at learning; their standing anomalies carry no action leverage. The model predicts where tolerated anomalies accumulate: in transparent-consequence environments, near decisions whose robust optimum is insensitive to the unresolved structure.' → 'At an irreparable record no expressible repair exists at any price (the anomaly is not merely unprofitable but impossible to fix within the current grammar), though the agent, unable to certify irreparability under noise, may keep electing while its deliberative record supports positive leverage and until the achievability weight decays.'
- C58 evidence pointer: 'paper/paper.md:L1192-L1275' → 'appendix/appendix.md#rem-irreparable'
- C59 text: 'Performance differences persist in this model not because an equilibrium concept freezes them but because the process that would erase them has died economically: the disadvantaged configuration generates no dissonance, or generates no leverage, or prices its question below the cost of asking.' → 'Terminal heterogeneity in awareness is path dependence in the strong sense: agents with identical preferences and identical Bayesian competence, facing the same objective system, end at different languages and different payoffs because their histories posed different questions.'
- C59 evidence pointer: 'paper/paper.md:L1289-L1356' → 'paper/paper.md:L1091-L1104'

The firms therefore commit to differentiated constant policies." → "Example 1 (continued, Section 6): Let beta = 1, with heterogeneous priors (firm 1 considers theta^0 more likely than theta^1, firm 2 the reverse). Against any conjecture, offering architecture 0 is subjectively strictly dominant for firm 1, and architecture 1 for firm 2, whenever mu_i(m^a) > (2 - beta)*mu_i(m^a'); at beta = 1 this is simple belief asymmetry, so the firms adopt differentiated constant policies. With T = 2 and two periods serving s_00 and s_11 under differentiated play, firm 1 wins at s_00 (payoffs 1, 0) and loses at s_11 (payoffs 0, 1). No constant assignment is consistent with both observations (m^0 fails at s_11, m^1 at s_00, and m^n predicts 1/2 at both), so each firm's active set empties: causal dissonance in its exact deterministic form. Had the firms pooled on the same architecture at both states, each would have received 1/2 every period, consistent with all three constant theories; the pooled profile is concealing. Belief heterogeneity, by inducing differentiated play, is what made the shared language's failure visible."

- C60 text: "The paper's absorbing configurations extend self-confirming equilibrium with two epistemic conditions—the language survives its own evidence, and no question is worth its price—and it is these conditions, not the strategic ones, that carry the new results." → 'Agents identical in preferences, competence, capacity, and cost can still end at different languages and payoffs, because their histories posed different questions and their truth-blind technologies produced different repairs: path dependence that begins before probability.'
- C60 evidence pointer: 'paper/paper.md:L1129-L1172' → 'paper/paper.md:L1091-L1104'
- C61 text: 'The distinction matters for how the theory-based view should treat “updating”: ranking the answers one can express and judging whether any expressible answer is adequate are different cognitive operations, and only the first is Bayesian.' → 'Agents identical in everything but their dissonance capacity $b_i$ and switching hurdle $\\kappa_i$ also diverge: in stochastic environments capacity affects detection speed and the robustness of rest points, while the hurdle sets the propensity to enlightened inertia; the capacity channel disappears under exact deterministic contradiction.'
- C61 evidence pointer: 'paper/paper.md:L503-L533' → 'paper/paper.md:L1089-L1104'
- C62 text: 'In the deterministic running example incoherence is a binary relation between labeled episodes and restoration is a graph-coloring problem; in general, a mixture of three mechanisms may defeat every single mechanism even though each pair of tokens is individually accommodatable, so restoration is a hypergraph coloring.' → 'Of the two origins of terminal heterogeneity, the first (different question histories and repairs) is the awareness-path microfoundation and the second (differences in dissonance capacity and switching hurdle) a parametric cognitive-style one, and neither is available in the fixed-language tradition.'

- C63 text: 'How much an environment lets agents price their own questions is itself a dimension of its diagnosticity, alongside exposure.' → 'Concealment is necessary, not sufficient: an objectively false theory that retains nonvanishing acting-belief mass in a stable SCUE must be $\\eta$-adequate on path, so the induced profile is concealing for that theory; shared false theories can remain viable when play pools states that a coarse language treats alike, but concealment alone does not guarantee survival.'

 - id: C1
   kind: contribution
   main: true
-  text: 'Existing models show how theories guide learning, how Bayesian agents discriminate among represented
-    causal structures, and how play can reveal previously unavailable actions. This paper models a different
-    transition: an agent''s causal language pools objectively different situations; strategic experience
-    endogenously reveals an action-relevant failure of that pooling and thereby creates a localized open
-    question; costly inquiry activates a fallible technology whose output is not measurable in the agent''s
-    prior awareness; only after that output is formulated does the agent construct a prior and undertake
-    Bayesian judgment, under an explicit account of how much confidence the generating experience licenses.
-    Because actions depend on theories, play can either create the contrasts that generate questions or
-    suppress them, so the model also delivers the population-level answer: when theory heterogeneity and
-    performance differences persist, and when the market erases them.'
+  text: 'Existing work explains learning and action once theories, vocabularies, or awareness advantages
+    exist; this paper supplies the pre-Bayesian passage from represented-class failure to a question,
+    a fallible new representation, formulation, and renewed Bayesian judgment. Its distinctive economic
+    and epistemic results are that an agent can value a finer actionable distinction before representing
+    its answers, and that evidence generating a truth-blind representation cannot be counted again as
+    independent validation. Embedding that passage in strategic interaction provides a mechanism by which
+    play exposes or conceals coarse languages and establishes the existence of absorbing configurations
+    with heterogeneous terminal languages and payoffs. The contribution is therefore upstream: question
+    histories and rational stopping points can generate strategic heterogeneity before priors over the
+    resulting theories are available. The paper endogenizes the strategically consequential transition
+    into a new hypothesis space while leaving the semantic act that produces it primitive.'
+  question: 'How an actor whose causal language has failed comes to possess a new one: the step before a
+    theory, or at least a hypothesis space, exists.'
+  question_pointer: paper/paper.md:L14-L15

+    worlds is accordingly finer than any single world requires; a technology that economizes on distinctions
+    must accept fallibility.'

+  text: 'Example 1 (continued, Section 7): After discovery the record labels architecture 0 as preferred

+    restorative, each separating s_00 from s_11: the row partition {{s_00,s_01},{s_10,s_11}}, the column
+    partition {{s_00,s_10},{s_01,s_11}}, and the two partitions isolating one labeled state. The record
+    directs inquiry (11 partitions are excluded) without determining it (4 remain). A factor-seeking technology
+    produces the row or the column refinement. The column world tau''(s_rc) = theta^c agrees with the
+    row world on the full joint mechanism at both visited states, so no truth-blind rule can formulate
+    correctly in both: a randomized rule succeeds in the worst case with probability at most 1/2. By Proposition
+    7.5 a partition sufficient for both worlds must be the full partition into singletons. Whichever factor
+    refinement arrives, its unique consistent theory extrapolates the wrong prediction to the two unlabeled
+    states in one of the two worlds.'

-- id: C12
-  kind: result

-  main: true
-  text: 'Proposition 7.4 (Answer invariance and the participation rule). B_i(r_i) is well defined, computable
-    from the deliberative record together with the known structure (Theta, u_i), and invariant across
-    the elements of R(P_i, r_i): every Lambda_i-measurable rule is implementable after any successful
-    production, and the maximand of equation (6) makes no reference to which repair arrives. Moreover
-    B_i(r_i) >= 0. Electing inquiry has represented value lambda_i B_i(r_i) - gamma_i, so agent i elects
-    if and only if lambda_i B_i(r_i) >= gamma_i; in particular, elections occur only at dissonant records
-    that admit some repair. Finally, B_i(r_i) > 0 if and only if some pinned rule strictly and robustly
-    improves on the carried-forward policy: questions whose every admissible answer supports current behavior
-    are worthless at any positive cost, however much model multiplicity remains.'
-  condition:
-  - B_i(r_i) is defined by equation (6) under the subjective recurrence premise at a dissonant record
-    with R(P_i, r_i) != emptyset, and B_i(r_i) = 0 otherwise
-  - gamma_i > 0
-  - lambda_i > 0
-  - lambda_i <= 1
-  - production is elective and dissonance-triggered (Assumption 7.1)
+  text: 'Proposition 8.4 (Participation): B_hat_i(r_i) is well defined, nonnegative, bounded by T*(max
+    pi_i - min pi_i), and computable from the deliberative record, the standing policy, and the agent-known
+    model primitives and test calibration. At a dissonant record, electing inquiry has represented value
+    lambda_i^(k_i)*B_hat_i(r_i) - gamma_i, so agent i elects if and only if lambda_i^(k_i)*B_hat_i(r_i)
+    >= gamma_i. Moreover, at a dissonant record, B_hat_i(r_i) > 0 if and only if J_i(G; r_i) > 0 for some
+    G in G_i(r_i). Thus a question is priced positively only when some coherent reading of the record
+    supports action variation within at least one current awareness cell that robustly outperforms the
+    best action kept uniform on that cell under the same coherent reading.'
+  condition: []

+- id: C12
+  kind: result
+  form: example
+  main: false
+  text: 'Example 1 (continued, Section 8): At the discovery record (T = 2, diagonal states served), deterministic
+    conflicting payoffs certify the two tokens as distinct states, and the best coherent grouping splits
+    them. The fine optimal gain is 0 on the winning token and 1/2 on the losing token, so L_i^fine = 1/2,
+    L_i^cur = 0, J_i = 1/2 and B_hat_i = (2/2)*(1/2) = 1/2 for both firms. Each elects iff lambda_i^(0)*B_hat_i
+    >= gamma_i, i.e. lambda_i^(0)/2 >= gamma_i. After the row refinement, the post-refinement horizon
+    gain is exactly 1/2, so the firm re-differentiates when kappa_i < 1/2 and holds its old policy (enlightened
+    inertia) when kappa_i >= 1/2. In a later stretch serving the two off-diagonal states, firm 1 holds
+    the row refinement and firm 2 the column refinement. Then L_1^fine = L_1^cur = J_1 = B_hat_1 = 0 (the
+    firm holding the truth never inquires again), while L_2^fine = 1, L_2^cur = 0, J_2 = 1 and B_hat_2
+    = (2/4)*1 = 1/2. The winner/loser asymmetry B_hat_1 = 0 < B_hat_2 is the durable point.'
+  condition: null
+  pointer: paper/paper.md#example-1-continued-4
+  evidence_pointer: paper/paper.md:L842-L870
+  derivation_id: D2

-  text: 'Example 1 (continued, section 7). At the dissonant record both firms'' committed constant policies
-    disagree with exactly one revealed label (firm 1 lost at s11, firm 2 at s00). Every restorative refinement
-    separates the two labeled tokens, and implementing a token''s revealed label at its recurrence gains
-    exactly one-half against every rival action: if the rival plays the winning architecture the switch
-    converts a differentiated loss (0) into a pooled half; if the rival switches, it converts a pooled
-    half into a differentiated win (1). Hence B_i = 1/2 for both firms, and each elects inquiry if and
-    only if lambda_i/2 >= gamma_i; the valuation uses no conjecture about the rival and no reference to
-    which of the four admissible refinements might arrive, Theta_i(t) is a singleton, and the bound is
-    exact. After a validation generation in which firm 1 has acquired the row refinement (certain of the
-    true row assignment) and firm 2 the column refinement (certain of the false column assignment), their
-    policies differ at the two off-diagonal states, firm 1 wins both, and the labels revealed there falsify
-    firm 2''s assignment: B_1 = 0, so the firm holding the truth never inquires again and its awareness
-    remains permanently incomplete; B_2 = 1, so firm 2 elects whenever lambda_2 >= gamma_2 and, upon success,
-    is driven stepwise to complete awareness.'

-  text: 'Theorem 8.2 (The insight is not evidence). If the technology is truth-blind, conditioning on
-    the production event adds nothing to the record: W equals the ordinary reflective posterior of m''''s
-    correctness given the record alone. Post-restoration fit is guaranteed by the restoration property,
-    so passing the test carries no information beyond the record either. Among candidates in T that induce
-    the same record distribution, prior odds are preserved exactly.'

+  text: 'Theorem 9.2 (No additional evidence beyond the generating record): Conditional on the analyst
+    record r_bar_i and the pre-production inquiry history h_i, a record-measurable, truth-blind production,
+    formulation, and bridge-prior-selection rule makes the complete output O_i = (P_i'', m'', mu_i^+)
+    conditionally independent of the candidate objective assignment. Consequently, under the reflective
+    joint law, for K_i(.|r_bar_i, h_i)-almost every realized output o and every tau'' in calT, nu(tau''
+    | r_bar_i, h_i, O_i = o) = nu(tau'' | r_bar_i, h_i), and therefore W(o) = nu(A_o | r_bar_i, h_i).
+    Passing the repair test adds no information beyond the record. If candidates assign equal likelihood
+    to (r_bar_i, h_i), their posterior odds remain their reflective prior odds.'

+  text: 'Corollary 9.3 (Self-audit requires sufficiency for the candidates): The assessment (calT, nu)
+    is expressible by agent i only if its current post-refinement partition P_i'' is sufficient for every
+    tau'' in calT, hence only if P_i'' refines the join of Proposition 7.5. This is necessary but not
+    sufficient for complete computation of warrant. An agent holding a minimal restoration therefore generally
+    cannot formulate the cross-representation comparison required to determine the sign or magnitude of
+    its signed warrant gap; a broader-awareness assessor may be able to.'

+  text: 'Example 1 (continued, Section 9): If firm i''s technology produces the column refinement, the
+    recoded record pins the unique consistent theory (architecture 0 on the first column, 1 on the second),
+    and representation-relative coherence assigns it probability 1. With calT = {row world, column world}
+    and a symmetric prior, the two worlds assign the realized diagonal-only record equal probability and
+    the technology is truth-blind. So by Theorem 9.2, W(O_i) = 1/2 and the signed warrant gap is 1 - 1/2
+    = +1/2. The same gap applies to a firm that acquires the row refinement (coherence 1, warrant 1/2):
+    confidence exceeds warrant even when the formulated theory is objectively correct. By Corollary 9.3
+    neither firm can conduct this assessment itself. One fresh differentiated observation at an off-diagonal
+    state resolves the row/column identification problem completely; at beta = 1, any number of pooled
+    observations remains nondiagnostic.'

+  pointer: paper/paper.md#example-1-continued-5

+  text: 'Theorem 10.4 (Epistemic absorption): Under Assumption 10.3, almost surely: (a) at most n*(|S|
+    - 1) refinements occur, and every agent''s awareness partition is eventually constant; (b) only finitely
+    many inquiry elections occur, because each agent stops electing once its achievability weight has
+    decayed enough that lambda_i^(k)*T*(max pi_i - min pi_i) < gamma_i, which happens after finitely many
+    consecutive failures because B_hat_i is bounded and gamma_i > 0; (c) there is an almost surely finite
+    period after which no agent''s language changes and no agent pays an inquiry cost, and every agent''s
+    epistemic life thereafter is judged-Bayesian updating within a fixed language. Moreover, on the event
+    that the standing profile is eventually constant: (d) every agent''s active set is eventually constant;
+    every retained theory is eta-adequate at every recurrent pair under terminal play; every theory eta-inadequate
+    at a recurrent pair is eventually absent; and each agent is eventually either never dissonant or in
+    dissonant stasis.'

+  text: 'Proposition D.7 (Capacity: detection speed and robustness), as stated in the paper: in stochastic
+    environments capacity is a single cognitive-style dial. Expected detection time under an exposed profile
+    is O(b_i/D_i) (and Omega(b_i)), while spurious dissonance at a non-exposed rest point has probability
+    at most exp(-b_i). A larger b_i lowers false-alarm risk but requires more accumulated evidence to
+    detect a broken language, and capacity plays no role in the exact deterministic case.'

-- id: C18
-  kind: result
-  form: proposition
-  main: true
-  text: 'Proposition 9.5 (Rest points and persistence). (i) If awareness partitions, committed policies,
-    conjectures, and acting beliefs are all eventually constant almost surely, then the limiting configuration,
-    restricted to the agents not in dissonant stasis, is a self-confirming unawareness equilibrium. (ii)
-    Conversely, consider a configuration whose epistemic states satisfy the SCUE conditions, whose play
-    generates only records with B_i = 0, and in which each agent''s committed action at each cell is strictly
-    optimal under every conjecture and every theory in its active set. (a) With deterministic mechanisms,
-    the configuration persists: dissonance, elections, and policy changes never occur, and the SCUE conditions
-    continue to hold, with conjectures converging empirically to induced play. (b) With stochastic mechanisms,
-    suppose in addition that every theory in every active set is eta''-adequate under the configuration''s
-    play for some eta'' < eta, and that the strict optimality extends to every (eta + epsilon)-adequate
-    theory for some epsilon in (0, 2(eta - eta'')]. Then for every delta > 0 there is a count threshold
-    N(delta) such that, if the configuration is reached with at least N(delta) observations at every recurrent
-    pair, then with probability at least 1 - delta play never changes, dissonance and elections never
-    occur, and the SCUE conditions hold in every subsequent generation, with conjectures converging empirically
-    to induced play.'
-  condition:
-  - '(i): awareness partitions, committed policies, conjectures and acting beliefs are all eventually
-    constant almost surely'
-  - '(i): Assumption 9.3 (the proof of (i) invokes Theorem 9.4(b) and (d))'
-  - '(ii): the epistemic states satisfy the SCUE conditions (i)-(iv) of Definition 9.1'
-  - '(ii): play generates only records with B_i = 0'
-  - '(ii): each agent''s committed action at each cell is strictly optimal under every conjecture and
-    every theory in its active set'
-  - '(ii-a): mechanisms are deterministic'
-  - '(ii-b): mechanisms are stochastic'
-  - '(ii-b): eta'' < eta'
-  - '(ii-b): every theory in every active set is eta''-adequate under the configuration''s play'
-  - '(ii-b): epsilon > 0'
-  - '(ii-b): epsilon <= 2*(eta - eta'')'
-  - '(ii-b): strict optimality extends to every (eta + epsilon)-adequate theory'
-  - '(ii-b): delta > 0'
-  - '(ii-b): the configuration is reached with at least N(delta) observations at every recurrent pair'

+    in which agents hold different terminal partitions and theories, and expected per-period payoffs differ
+    strictly and permanently, with no disadvantaged agent closing the gap. This is because, for each,
+    the corrective process has died economically at one of its two stages: either its question is unpriced
+    (dissonant stasis, lambda_i^(k_i)*B_hat_i(r_i) < gamma_i from some period on), or, having asked and
+    refined, the policy change its new model recommends is priced below the switching hurdle (enlightened
+    inertia, T-horizon gain <= kappa_i).'

-  text: 'Example 1 (continued, section 9, the trap and its knife edge). Set beta = 1 and suppose both
-    firms acquired the column refinement after the discovery generation and became certain of the false
-    column assignment. Each then plays the column policy; the firms pool at every state; every payoff
-    is one-half; and one-half is exactly what the column theory predicts under pooled play. The configuration
-    is a SCUE with payoff-relevant error: policies are subjectively optimal, conjectures confirmed, no
-    dissonance ever occurs (the profile is concealing), and B_i = 0 for both firms, so the configuration
-    survives even a free, infallible inquiry technology; the warrant gap is permanent, each firm remaining
-    exactly half-warranted as unbounded experience accumulates. Now let beta < 1: in the first generation
-    containing an off-diagonal state, the pooled firms receive beta/2 where their theory predicts one-half;
-    the demand side reveals the label, dissonance returns, leverage turns positive, and the reflective
-    posterior becomes degenerate. Payoff-relevant self-confirmation is a knife-edge property of perfectly
-    forgiving demand (the beta < 1 environments are diagnostic), and the destroyed value 1 - beta at each
-    misserved episode is itself the corrective signal.'
+  text: 'Example 1 (continued, Section 10). The trap: at beta = 1, if both firms acquired the column refinement
+    and became certain of the false column assignment, they pool at every state and every payoff is 1/2,
+    exactly what the column theory predicts. The configuration is a SCUE with payoff-relevant error, and
+    the positive signed warrant gap is permanent: each firm remains exactly half-warranted. The knife
+    edge: for beta < 1, the first period serving an off-diagonal state pays beta/2 where the theory predicts
+    1/2, so dissonance returns, B_hat turns positive and the reflective posterior becomes degenerate;
+    payoff-relevant self-confirmation is a knife-edge property of perfectly forgiving demand. Persistence:
+    at beta < 1, firm 1 holds the row refinement and the true assignment while firm 2 stays coarse and
+    is dissonant from t_0. If gamma_2 > lambda_2^(k_{2,t_0})*B_hat_2(r_{2,t}) for every subsequent record
+    (for which gamma_2 > lambda_2^(k_{2,t_0})*T*(max pi_2 - min pi_2) suffices), firm 2 never elects and
+    is in dissonant stasis. Its balanced four-token record with T = 2 gives L_2^fine = 1, L_2^cur = 0,
+    J_2 = 1 and B_hat_2 = 1/2, and under uniform rho the firms earn 3/4 and 1/4 per period, permanently.
+    At beta = 1 the continuation is either a one-switch path into renewed dissonance or a stable policy-inertia
+    path, not an indefinite oscillation.'

 - id: C25
   kind: result
   main: false
-  text: 'When every represented theory is false, the posterior does not sound an alarm: by the logic of
-    misspecified Bayesian learning (Berk 1966), it concentrates on the least wrong of the available
-    theories, and the agent grows more confident, not less; within-language updating is self-sealing. In
-    the stochastic setting (Remark 4.2), when every expressible theory is false because the agent''s
-    cell mixes mechanisms, a full-support posterior does not signal this: it concentrates on the
-    expressible theories closest to the on-path conditionals in Kullback–Leibler divergence, and the
-    agent becomes increasingly *confident* in a wrong theory. Posterior mass always sums to one; the
-    inadequacy of the whole model class is not an event in the agent''s algebra, so detecting that the
-    language itself is broken requires a faculty outside the posterior—the consistency test.'
-  condition:

-  text: 'The return to Bayes is disciplined by a warrant account: because any successful repair fits the
-    experience that generated it by construction, retrospective fit carries no information, and—when
-    inquiry is truth-blind—neither does the arrival of the insight itself. Post-insight confidence
-    therefore exceeds warrant by a computable gap, and an agent holding a minimal repair generally
-    cannot even formulate the comparison that would measure its own gap: the agent returns to the
-    Bayesian world carrying a warrant gap of known location and generally unknowable (to it) size.'

+    likelihood differs across relevant candidate worlds, and fresh evidence changes reflective warrant
+    only when its likelihood differs across the represented candidate worlds; otherwise further
+    awareness expansion may be required before the agent can use the evidence for that comparison.
+    Because the agent may lack the awareness needed to formulate that comparison, this gives boards,
+    investors, and other broader-awareness outsiders a precise epistemic role that retrospective

-  text: 'Because theories cause actions and actions generate the evidence, almost surely awareness
-    change and inquiry die out, leaving every agent permanently inhabiting a Bayesian world—models fit,
-    questions are dead, inquiry is priced below cost. The configurations in which the process rests,
-    self-confirming unawareness equilibria, can preserve false theories, heterogeneous awareness
-    (heterogeneous causal languages), and permanent performance differences, and they do so precisely
-    when neither of the two corrective forces—strategic variety inside an agent''s categories, or an
-    environment harsh enough to punish misservice observably—is present: heterogeneous causal languages,
-    and the performance differences they support, persist exactly when these strategic and environmental
-    conditions fail to erase them.'

+  text: 'The terminal analysis adds two epistemic possibilities to the fixed-hypothesis-space models:
+    self-confirming unawareness equilibrium requires a surviving represented class and no worthwhile
+    question, while dissonant stasis permits rational cessation outside a Bayesian world; enlightened
+    inertia adds a policy condition: even after an improved policy becomes representable, its
+    finite-horizon gain may not clear the switching hurdle. Together these conditions show how
+    restoration of a viable represented class, termination of inquiry, and continuation of play can
+    diverge.'

+  text: 'Every dissonant record is repairable under the exact calibration but not in general. With
+    deterministic mechanisms every dissonant record is repairable, and finiteness of $\Pi(S)$ ensures a
+    minimally restorative refinement exists; existence is guaranteed only in the deterministic case.
+    With stochastic mechanisms, multiple observations at a single state may yield empirical conditionals
+    for which no one mechanism satisfies equation (6) across the observed profiles; because no
+    refinement can split a state, such a record has $\mathcal R(\mathcal P_i,\bar r_i)=\emptysetset$ and
+    is irreparable: dissonance names a defect that no expressible refinement can repair.'

-  text: 'Strategic variety is traced to its source: agents with identical preferences, identical
-    data-processing (Bayesian) competence, and identical objective opportunities, facing the same
-    objective system, end up with different causal languages, different theories, and different payoffs
-    because their experiences posed different questions, their technologies produced different repairs,
-    and their subsequent positions extinguished further inquiry at different points. Terminal
-    heterogeneity in awareness is path dependence in the strong sense, and this is a microfoundation for
-    heterogeneity that begins before probability does: why similar firms perform differently receives an
-    answer that begins before beliefs—because they came to different questions, and some questions died
-    before they were asked.'

-  text: 'Remark 6.3 (Irreparable records). With deterministic mechanisms, restoration is always
-    possible: the singleton partition recodes each token to its own state, where the generating
-    mechanism fits exactly. With stochastic mechanisms this guarantee fails: an unlucky finite sample at
-    a single state can produce an empirical conditional outside the tolerance of every mechanism, and no
-    partition of $S$ can separate observations generated at the same state; then $\mathcal R(\mathcal
-    P_i,r_i)=\emptysetset$ and the record is irreparable. Because $\mathcal R$ is computable from the
-    record, the agent recognizes irreparability; the technology fails surely; and the participation rule
-    assigns the question no value, so no inquiry cost is wasted on it. Under recurring visitation
-    irreparability is transient, but a record whose critical pairs stop being visited can remain
-    irreparable forever.'
-  condition: null

-    the bound is exact; the laboratory''s leverage of one-half is this case. When consequences conceal
-    forgone values, $\Theta_i(t)$ is large, the minimum drags each term toward zero, and $B_i$
-    understates the objective value of repair: opaque environments rationally suppress inquiry.'

 - id: C53
   kind: result
   main: false
-  text: 'Remark 9.7 (Rational anomaly tolerance). Zero-leverage dissonant stasis is a recognizable
-    condition, not a pathology of the model: the agent knows its causal account is broken—every
-    expressible theory is rejected—yet the break disturbs no represented action comparison, so repairing
-    it is worth nothing; anomaly tolerance is rational precisely when pinned distinctions carry no
-    robust action leverage. At an irreparable record, $\mathcal R=\emptysetset$, no expressible repair
-    exists at any price, and the anomaly is not merely unprofitable to fix but impossible to fix within
-    the current grammar.'
-  condition: null

 - id: C54
   kind: causal
   main: false
-  text: 'Strategic variety has a pre-probabilistic source: agents with identical preferences, identical
-    Bayesian competence, and identical objective opportunities diverge because their experiences posed
-    different questions, their technologies produced different repairs, and their positions extinguished
-    inquiry at different points. Heterogeneous prior understanding generates heterogeneous questions;
-    questions generate heterogeneous languages; languages generate heterogeneous theories, plans, and
-    payoffs. This sequence begins before probability does, and it is path-dependent in the strong sense:
-    the order of experiences shapes the terminal language, and agents can converge to the same behavior
-    while retaining permanently different representations.'
-  condition: null
-  pointer: paper/paper.md:L1366-L1377
-  evidence_pointer: paper/paper.md:L1289-L1356

+    band absorbs belief fluctuations too small to be worth acting on, so only swings whose horizon-$T$
+    gain exceeds $\kappa_i$ move play, a stabilizing force, though not one that rules out
+    large-amplitude cycles.'

 - id: C55
   kind: result
   main: false
-  text: 'The value of experimentation is not information: a question is worth asking when some
-    admissible answer would change action, not when its resolution would be most informative.
-    High-entropy uncertainty with no action leverage is rationally ignored, and narrow contrasts that
-    straddle an action threshold are rationally pursued. This explains rational cessation—why successful
-    firms stop asking—and the loser''s advantage in inquiry: competitive defeat concentrates the
-    evidence, the dissonance, and the incentive in the defeated firm.'

-  text: 'Early confidence outruns evidence even when correct. A founder''s theory that beautifully

-    differs across the candidate worlds, and, by Corollary 8.3, the founder typically cannot even
-    express the comparison, which is a formal argument for outside challenge (boards, investors,
-    advisors with different awareness) as a warrant technology rather than a monitoring technology.'

 - id: C57
   kind: result
   main: false
-  text: 'Markets discipline theories through two channels: payoff-relevant collective error requires
-    both concealment by play and forgiveness by demand. Industries where customers absorb
-    misservice—captive buyers, no substitutes, third parties bearing the cost—can sustain shared false
-    theories indefinitely; industries with strong outside options discipline theories even under
-    consensus. Where demand is forgiving, the only corrective is strategic variety in beliefs, which
-    gives belief heterogeneity a social value distinct from its private value: the entrant with a
-    deviant theory is the industry''s epistemic infrastructure.'
-  condition: null

+- id: C70

+  text: 'The same logic integrates anomaly tolerance and cessation: a firm may rationally leave a

+- id: C71

+  main: false
+  text: 'Success can remove action-separating leverage, repeated production failure can reduce the
+    assessed chance of resolution, and a genuine anomaly can remain economically dormant when no
+    feasible refinement changes robust action. Questions can therefore die through success,
+    discouragement, or irreparability even though the underlying causal structure remains unresolved.'
